+++ b/src/components/StudentBook/ContractPencilCursor.tsx
@@ -0,0 +1,163 @@
+import { useEffect, useId, useRef } from "react";
+import { createPortal } from "react-dom";
+import { PencilShape } from "@/components/cartilla/WorkbookPencilMark";
+import { recordPointer } from "@/lib/cursor-mode";
+import {
+  contractEase,
+  contractTipAt,
+  getTipFlight,
+  isEraserActive,
+  markToScreen,
+  PENCIL_CONTRACT,
+  subscribeTip,
+} from "@/lib/pencil-contract";
+
+/** Size of the close-up window around the graphite point (CSS px). */
+const FINE_SIZE = 168;
+const COARSE_SIZE = 128;
+/** The graphite point sits this far inside the window so the tip is never clipped. */
+const PAD = 6;
+
+/**
+ * Candidate B — the single Large Realistic Wooden Pencil-Tip cursor (November 1
+ * contract). It is the existing Workbook PencilShape, shown close up with its
+ * graphite point as the hotspot, pointing up-left. It follows the real pointer,
+ * travels along each mark while the kernel draws it, and hides while the eraser
+ * works. Purely visual: pointer events always reach the real controls.
+ */
+export function ContractPencilCursor() {
+  const host = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
+  const uid = `contract-tip-${useId().replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g, "")}`;
+  const coarse =
+    typeof window !== "undefined" && !!window.matchMedia?.("(pointer: coarse)").matches;
+  const size = coarse ? COARSE_SIZE : FINE_SIZE;
+
+  useEffect(() => {
+    const root = document.documentElement;
+    root.classList.add("cartilla-cursor-contract");
+    let pointer: [number, number] | null = null;
+    let inside = false;
+    let pressed = false;
+    let returnFrom: { at: number; pt: [number, number] } | null = null;
+    let lastShown: [number, number] | null = null;
+    let raf = 0;
+
+    const place = (x: number, y: number, visible: boolean) => {
+      const el = host.current;
+      if (!el) return;
+      el.style.transform = `translate3d(${(x - PAD).toFixed(1)}px, ${(y - PAD).toFixed(1)}px, 0)`;
+      el.dataset.visible = visible ? "true" : "false";
+      if (visible) lastShown = [x, y];
+    };
+
+    const frame = () => {
+      raf = requestAnimationFrame(frame);
+      const now = performance.now();
+      const erasing = isEraserActive();
+      const flight = getTipFlight();
+      if (flight && flight.el.isConnected) {
+        const ms = now - flight.start;
+        const first = flight.strokes[0];
+        const tip = contractTipAt(flight.strokes, ms);
+        let target: [number, number];
+        if (tip) target = markToScreen(flight.el, flight.height, tip);
+        else {
+          const startPt = markToScreen(flight.el, flight.height, first.points[0]);
+          const from = lastShown ?? pointer ?? startPt;
+          const k = contractEase(ms / Math.max(1, first.begin));
+          target = [from[0] + (startPt[0] - from[0]) * k, from[1] + (startPt[1] - from[1]) * k];
+        }
+        returnFrom = { at: now, pt: target };
+        place(target[0], target[1], !erasing);
+        return;
+      }
+      if (returnFrom && pointer && now - returnFrom.at < 300) {
+        const k = contractEase((now - returnFrom.at) / 300);
+        place(
+          returnFrom.pt[0] + (pointer[0] - returnFrom.pt[0]) * k,
+          returnFrom.pt[1] + (pointer[1] - returnFrom.pt[1]) * k,
+          !erasing,
+        );
+        return;
+      }
+      if (pointer) place(pointer[0], pointer[1], !erasing && inside && (!coarse || pressed));
+      else if (host.current) host.current.dataset.visible = "false";
+    };
+
+    const move = (event: PointerEvent) => {
+      recordPointer(event.clientX, event.clientY);
+      pointer = [event.clientX, event.clientY];
+      const target = event.target instanceof Element ? event.target : null;
+      inside =
+        !!target?.closest(".native-lesson-viewer") &&
+        !target.closest("input, textarea, select, [contenteditable=true], .native-lesson-viewer__nav");
+    };
+    const down = (event: PointerEvent) => {
+      pressed = true;
+      move(event);
+    };
+    const up = () => {
+      pressed = false;
+    };
+    const leave = () => {
+      inside = false;
+      pressed = false;
+    };
+    const unsubscribe = subscribeTip(() => undefined);
+    window.addEventListener("pointermove", move, true);
+    window.addEventListener("pointerdown", down, true);
+    window.addEventListener("pointerup", up, true);
+    window.addEventListener("pointercancel", leave, true);
+    document.addEventListener("pointerleave", leave);
+    window.addEventListener("blur", leave);
+    raf = requestAnimationFrame(frame);
+    return () => {
+      cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
+      unsubscribe();
+      root.classList.remove("cartilla-cursor-contract");
+      window.removeEventListener("pointermove", move, true);
+      window.removeEventListener("pointerdown", down, true);
+      window.removeEventListener("pointerup", up, true);
+      window.removeEventListener("pointercancel", leave, true);
+      document.removeEventListener("pointerleave", leave);
+      window.removeEventListener("blur", leave);
+    };
+  }, [coarse]);
+
+  if (typeof document === "undefined") return null;
+  // Tip at (PAD, PAD); body runs down-right (rotate 135° turns the shape's -y body axis).
+  const scale = coarse ? 2.6 : 3.4;
+  return createPortal(
+    <div
+      ref={host}
+      className="contract-pencil-cursor"
+      data-visible="false"
+      data-testid="contract-pencil-cursor"
+      aria-hidden="true"
+      style={{ width: size, height: size }}
+    >
+      <svg width={size} height={size} viewBox={`0 0 ${size} ${size}`} overflow="hidden">
+        <defs>
+          <linearGradient id={`${uid}-fade`} x1="0" y1="0" x2="1" y2="1">
+            <stop offset="0.55" stopColor="#fff" />
+            <stop offset="1" stopColor="#fff" stopOpacity="0" />
+          </linearGradient>
+          <mask id={`${uid}-mask`} maskUnits="userSpaceOnUse" x="0" y="0" width={size} height={size}>
+            <rect width={size} height={size} fill={`url(#${uid}-fade)`} />
+          </mask>
+          <filter id={`${uid}-shadow`} x="-20%" y="-20%" width="160%" height="160%">
+            <feDropShadow dx="2.5" dy="3.5" stdDeviation="2.5" floodColor="#3b2a14" floodOpacity="0.28" />
+          </filter>
+        </defs>
+        <g mask={`url(#${uid}-mask)`} filter={`url(#${uid}-shadow)`}>
+          <g transform={`translate(${PAD} ${PAD}) rotate(135) scale(${scale})`}>
+            <PencilShape uid={uid} />
+          </g>
+        </g>
+      </svg>
+    </div>,
+    document.body,
+  );
+}
+
+export const CONTRACT_CURSOR_TIMING = PENCIL_CONTRACT;

+/** Candidate B: one short pencil piece; its width follows the pencil speed. */
+function ContractSegment({
+  d,
+  width,
+  begin,
+  dur,
+  animate,
+}: {
+  d: string;
+  width: number;
+  begin: number;
+  dur: number;
+  animate: boolean;
+}) {
+  const style = { strokeWidth: `${width}px` };
+  if (!animate)
+    return <path className="workbook-pencil-mark__stroke" d={d} style={style} vectorEffect="non-scaling-stroke" />;
+  return (
+    <path
+      className="workbook-pencil-mark__stroke"
+      d={d}
+      style={style}
+      vectorEffect="non-scaling-stroke"
+      pathLength={100}
+      strokeDasharray="100 100"
+      strokeDashoffset="100"
+      opacity="0"
+    >
+      <animate attributeName="stroke-dashoffset" begin={`${begin}ms`} dur={`${dur}ms`} fill="freeze" values="100;0" />
+      <set attributeName="opacity" to="1" begin={`${begin}ms`} fill="freeze" />
+    </path>
+  );

+import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";
+import {
+  download,
+  historyToCsv,
+  historyToJson,
+  importHistory,
+  latestDecisions,
+  loadHistory,
+  recordDecision,
+  type DecisionRecord,
+  type OwnerDecision,
+} from "@/lib/owner-review-store";
+import {
+  AREA_STATUS,
+  COMPARISONS,
+  DOWNLOADS,
+  QUICK_LINKS,
+  REVIEW_REVISION,
+  type Comparison,
+  type OwnerStatus,
+} from "./review-data";
+import "@/styles/owner-review.css";
+
+const DECISION_LABEL: Record<OwnerDecision, string> = {
+  "approve-a": "Approve A",
+  "approve-b": "Approve B",
+  "reject-both": "Reject both",
+  "request-changes": "Request changes",
+  "keep-current": "Keep current",
+  "upload-replacement": "Upload owner replacement",
+  "accept-original": "Accept original",
+  "accept-alternate": "Accept alternate",
+  "needs-rerecording": "Needs authentic re-recording",
+  reject: "Reject",
+};
+
+function useHistory() {
+  const [history, setHistory] = useState<DecisionRecord[]>([]);
+  useEffect(() => setHistory(loadHistory()), []);
+  return [history, setHistory] as const;
+}
+
+function DecisionBar({
+  itemId,
+  options,
+  history,
+  onChange,
+}: {
+  itemId: string;
+  options: OwnerDecision[];
+  history: DecisionRecord[];
+  onChange: (h: DecisionRecord[]) => void;
+}) {
+  const [comment, setComment] = useState("");
+  const latest = latestDecisions(history).get(itemId);
+  const commentId = `${itemId}-comment`;
+  const save = (decision: OwnerDecision) => {
+    if (decision === "request-changes" && !comment.trim()) return;
+    const note = comment.trim();
+    onChange(recordDecision({ itemId, decision, comment: note, revision: REVIEW_REVISION }));
+    setComment("");
+  };
+  return (
+    <div className="orh-decision" data-item={itemId}>
+      <p className="orh-decision__state" aria-live="polite">
+        {latest ? (
+          <>
+            Your decision: <b>{DECISION_LABEL[latest.decision]}</b>
+            {latest.comment ? ` — “${latest.comment}”` : ""}{" "}
+            <span className="orh-muted">({new Date(latest.at).toLocaleString()})</span>
+          </>
+        ) : (
+          <>No decision yet. Opening or trying a version does not count as approval.</>
+        )}
+      </p>
+      <label htmlFor={commentId} className="orh-label">
+        Comments (required for “Request changes”)
+      </label>
+      <textarea
+        id={commentId}
+        value={comment}
+        onChange={(e) => setComment(e.target.value)}
+        rows={2}
+        className="orh-textarea"
+      />
+      <p className="orh-muted">Replacement file upload is not available in this draft.</p>
+      <div className="orh-decision__buttons">
+        {options.map((o) => (
+          <button
+            key={o}
+            type="button"
+            className={`orh-btn orh-btn--${o}`}
+            onClick={() => save(o)}
+            disabled={(o === "request-changes" && !comment.trim())}
+          >
+            {DECISION_LABEL[o]}
+          </button>
+        ))}
+      </div>
+    </div>
+  );
+}
+
+function Links({ links }: { links: { label: string; href: string }[] }) {
+  return (
+    <ul className="orh-links">
+      {links.map((l) => (
+        <li key={l.href}>
+          <a href={l.href} target={l.href.startsWith("/review/") ? "_blank" : undefined} rel="noreferrer">
+            {l.label}
+          </a>
+        </li>
+      ))}
+    </ul>
+  );
+}
+
+function ComparisonCard({
+  c,
+  history,
+  onChange,
+}: {
+  c: Comparison;
+  history: DecisionRecord[];
+  onChange: (h: DecisionRecord[]) => void;
+}) {
+  const options: OwnerDecision[] = c.b
+    ? ["approve-a", "approve-b", "reject-both", "request-changes"]
+    : ["keep-current", "reject", "request-changes"];
+  if (c.artwork) options.push("keep-current");
+  return (
+    <article className="orh-card" id={c.id}>
+      <h3>{c.title}</h3>
+      <p>{c.summary}</p>
+      <div className={c.b ? "orh-ab" : "orh-single"}>
+        {[c.a, c.b].filter(Boolean).map((v) => (
+          <section key={v!.name} className="orh-variant">
+            <h4>{v!.name}</h4>
+            <p>{v!.description}</p>
+            <Links links={v!.links} />
+          </section>
+        ))}
+      </div>
+      {c.singleOptionReason && <p className="orh-note">{c.singleOptionReason}</p>}
+      <details className="orh-details">
+        <summary>Proof (screenshots and recordings)</summary>
+        <Links links={c.evidence} />
+      </details>
+      <DecisionBar itemId={c.id} options={options} history={history} onChange={onChange} />
+    </article>
+  );
+}
+
+type Row = Record<string, string>;
+function useJson<T>(url: string): T | null {
+  const [data, setData] = useState<T | null>(null);
+  useEffect(() => {
+    let live = true;
+    fetch(url)
+      .then((r) => (r.ok ? (r.json() as Promise<T>) : null))
+      .then((d) => live && setData(d))
+      .catch(() => live && setData(null));
+    return () => {
+      live = false;
+    };
+  }, [url]);
+  return data;
+}
+
+const STATUS_CLASS: Record<OwnerStatus, string> = {
+  "DONE and live": "live",
+  "DONE but not live": "ready",
+  WAITING: "waiting",
+  "NOT TESTED": "untested",
+};
+
+function ArtworkBoard({ history, onChange }: { history: DecisionRecord[]; onChange: (h: DecisionRecord[]) => void }) {
+  const data = useJson<{ summary: Row; issues: Row[] }>("/review/artwork-board.json");
+  const [filter, setFilter] = useState("all");
+  if (!data) return <p className="orh-muted">Artwork review data is not available in this draft.</p>;
+  const rows = data.issues.filter((r) => filter === "all" || r.classification === filter);
+  const kinds = Array.from(new Set(data.issues.map((r) => r.classification)));
+  return (
+    <div>
+      <dl className="orh-summary">
+        {Object.entries(data.summary).map(([k, v]) => (
+          <div key={k}>
+            <dt>{k}</dt>
+            <dd>{v}</dd>
+          </div>
+        ))}
+      </dl>
+      <label className="orh-label">
+        Show{" "}
+        <select value={filter} onChange={(e) => setFilter(e.target.value)}>
+          <option value="all">all ({data.issues.length})</option>
+          {kinds.map((k) => (
+            <option key={k} value={k}>
+              {k} ({data.issues.filter((r) => r.classification === k).length})
+            </option>
+          ))}
+        </select>
+      </label>
+      <div className="orh-art-list">
+        {rows.slice(0, 60).map((r) => (
+          <article key={r.id} className="orh-art" id={`art-${r.id}`}>
+            <div className="orh-art__images">
+              {r.pageImage && (
+                <figure>
+                  <img src={r.pageImage} alt={`Printed page ${r.page}`} loading="lazy" />
+                  <figcaption>Printed page {r.page}</figcaption>
+                </figure>
+              )}
+              {r.servedPath && (
+                <figure>
+                  <img
+                    src={r.servedPath}
+                    alt={`Current image ${r.id}`}
+                    loading="lazy"
+                    style={{ width: r.displayWidth ? `${Math.min(220, Number(r.displayWidth))}px` : undefined }}
+                  />
+                  <figcaption>
+                    Current: {r.width}×{r.height}px
+                  </figcaption>
+                </figure>
+              )}
+            </div>
+            <p>
+              <b>{r.id}</b> · page {r.page} · <code>{r.servedPath}</code>
+            </p>
+            <p>
+              <b>{r.classification}</b> — {r.problem}
+            </p>
+            <p className="orh-muted">Proposed: {r.remedy}</p>
+            <DecisionBar
+              itemId={`art:${r.id}`}
+              options={["keep-current", "request-changes", "reject"]}
+              history={history}
+              onChange={onChange}
+            />
+          </article>
+        ))}
+      </div>
+      {rows.length > 60 && <p className="orh-muted">Showing 60 of {rows.length}. Full list in the CSV downloads.</p>}
+    </div>
+  );
+}
+
+function AudioBoard({ history, onChange }: { history: DecisionRecord[]; onChange: (h: DecisionRecord[]) => void }) {
+  const data = useJson<{ summary: Row; rows: Row[] }>("/review/audio-board.json");
+  const [filter, setFilter] = useState("MISSING");
+  if (!data) return <p className="orh-muted">Audio review data is not available in this draft.</p>;
+  const kinds = Array.from(new Set(data.rows.map((r) => r.classification)));
+  const rows = data.rows.filter((r) => filter === "all" || r.classification === filter);
+  return (
+    <div>
+      <dl className="orh-summary">
+        {Object.entries(data.summary).map(([k, v]) => (
+          <div key={k}>
+            <dt>{k}</dt>
+            <dd>{v}</dd>
+          </div>
+        ))}
+      </dl>
+      <label className="orh-label">
+        Show{" "}
+        <select value={filter} onChange={(e) => setFilter(e.target.value)}>
+          <option value="all">all ({data.rows.length})</option>
+          {kinds.map((k) => (
+            <option key={k} value={k}>
+              {k} ({data.rows.filter((r) => r.classification === k).length})
+            </option>
+          ))}
+        </select>
+      </label>
+      <div className="orh-audio-list">
+        {rows.slice(0, 80).map((r) => (
+          <article key={r.id} className="orh-audio">
+            <p>
+              <b lang="es">{r.script}</b> · {r.category} · {r.where}
+            </p>
+            {r.file ? (
+              <audio controls preload="none" src={r.file}>
+                <track kind="captions" />
+              </audio>
+            ) : (
+              <p className="orh-missing">No authentic recording — stays silent.</p>
+            )}
+            <p className="orh-muted">
+              {r.classification} — {r.note}
+            </p>
+            <DecisionBar
+              itemId={`audio:${r.id}`}
+              options={["accept-original", "accept-alternate", "needs-rerecording", "reject"]}
+              history={history}
+              onChange={onChange}
+            />
+          </article>
+        ))}
+      </div>
+      {rows.length > 80 && <p className="orh-muted">Showing 80 of {rows.length}. Full list in the CSV downloads.</p>}
+    </div>
+  );
+}
+

+          <span className="orh-muted"> {g.note}</span>
+        </li>
+      ))}
+    </ul>
+  );
+}

+              const f = e.target.files?.[0];

+              try {

+                setMsg("That file is not a review export.");

+            }}
+          />
+        </div>
+        <p aria-live="polite">{msg}</p>
+        <ol className="orh-history">
+          {history
+            .slice()
+            .reverse()
+            .map((r) => (
+              <li key={`${r.itemId}-${r.at}`}>
+                {new Date(r.at).toLocaleString()} — <b>{r.itemId}</b>: {DECISION_LABEL[r.decision]}
+                {r.comment ? ` — “${r.comment}”` : ""}
+              </li>
+            ))}
+        </ol>
+      </section>
+    </main>
+  );
+}

new file mode 100644
index 00000000..a96150ac
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/lib/__tests__/owner-review-store.test.ts

+import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
+import {
+  historyToCsv,
+  importHistory,
+  latestDecisions,
+  loadHistory,
+  OWNER_REVIEW_BACKUP_KEY,
+  OWNER_REVIEW_KEY,
+  recordDecision,
+} from "@/lib/owner-review-store";
+
+describe("owner review store", () => {
+  beforeEach(() => localStorage.clear());
+
+  it("starts empty: nothing is approved by default", () => {
+    expect(loadHistory()).toEqual([]);
+  });
+
+  it("keeps an append-only history and the latest decision per item", () => {
+    recordDecision({ itemId: "wb-l3", decision: "approve-a", comment: "", revision: "x" });
+    recordDecision({ itemId: "wb-l3", decision: "request-changes", comment: "más grande", revision: "x" });
+    const history = loadHistory();
+    expect(history).toHaveLength(2);
+    expect(latestDecisions(history).get("wb-l3")?.decision).toBe("request-changes");
+    expect(historyToCsv(history)).toContain('"más grande"');
+  });
+
+  it("recovers from the backup copy when the main copy is corrupted", () => {
+    recordDecision({ itemId: "a", decision: "approve-b", comment: "", revision: "x" });
+    recordDecision({ itemId: "b", decision: "reject-both", comment: "", revision: "x" });
+    localStorage.setItem(OWNER_REVIEW_KEY, "{broken");
+    expect(localStorage.getItem(OWNER_REVIEW_BACKUP_KEY)).toBeTruthy();
+    expect(loadHistory().map((r) => r.itemId)).toEqual(["a"]);
+  });
+
+  it("restores an exported file without duplicating entries", () => {
+    const h = recordDecision({ itemId: "a", decision: "approve-b", comment: "", revision: "x" });
+    const json = JSON.stringify({ version: 1, history: h });
+    localStorage.clear();
+    expect(importHistory(json)).toHaveLength(1);
+    expect(importHistory(json)).toHaveLength(1);
+    expect(() => importHistory("{}")).toThrow();
+  });
+});

+import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
+import { contractTipAt, PENCIL_CONTRACT, velocitySegments, type ContractStroke } from "@/lib/pencil-contract";
+import { markInProgress } from "@/components/StudentBook/NativeLessonViewer";
+
+describe("contract candidate and turn guard", () => {
+  it("uses the intended durations and bounds stroke widths", () => {
+    expect(PENCIL_CONTRACT.holdMs).toBe(400);
+    expect(PENCIL_CONTRACT.eraseMs).toBe(3500);
+    expect(PENCIL_CONTRACT.crumbCount).toBe(12);
+    const stroke: ContractStroke = { points: [[0, 0], [100, 0]], begin: 300, dur: 2000 };
+    const segments = velocitySegments(stroke);
+    expect(segments).toHaveLength(14);
+    expect(segments.every(s => s.width >= 3.3 && s.width <= 6.4)).toBe(true);
+    expect(contractTipAt([stroke], 1300)?.[0]).toBeCloseTo(50);
+  });
+  it.each(["drawing", "holding", "erasing"])("blocks a turn while %s", status => {
+    const root = document.createElement("div");
+    root.innerHTML = `<span class="workbook-pencil-mark" data-mark-status="${status}"></span>`;
+    expect(markInProgress(root)).toBe(true);
+  });
+  it("does not block on a completed mark", () => {
+    const root = document.createElement("div");
+    root.innerHTML = '<span class="workbook-pencil-mark" data-mark-status="correct"></span>';
+    expect(markInProgress(root)).toBe(false);
+  });
+});

diff --git a/src/lib/owner-review-store.ts b/src/lib/owner-review-store.ts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..adc6ec79
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/lib/owner-review-store.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,110 @@
+/**
+ * Owner review decisions for the November 16 review hub.
+ * Stored only in this browser (same mechanism as the demo class), with an
+ * append-only history plus JSON/CSV export and JSON restore. Nothing is ever
+ * recorded on the owner's behalf: a decision exists only after he presses a button.
+ */
+export type OwnerDecision =
+  | "approve-a"
+  | "approve-b"
+  | "reject-both"
+  | "request-changes"
+  | "keep-current"
+  | "upload-replacement"
+  | "accept-original"
+  | "accept-alternate"
+  | "needs-rerecording"
+  | "reject";
+
+export interface DecisionRecord {
+  itemId: string;
+  decision: OwnerDecision;
+  comment: string;
+  at: string;
+  /** Revision of the review build the owner was looking at. */
+  revision: string;
+}
+
+export const OWNER_REVIEW_KEY = "cartilla:owner-review:v1";
+export const OWNER_REVIEW_BACKUP_KEY = "cartilla:owner-review:v1:backup";
+
+interface Stored {
+  version: 1;
+  history: DecisionRecord[];
+}
+
+function read(key: string): Stored | null {
+  try {
+    const raw = localStorage.getItem(key);
+    if (!raw) return null;
+    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw) as Stored;
+    return parsed && parsed.version === 1 && Array.isArray(parsed.history) ? parsed : null;
+  } catch {
+    return null;
+  }
+}
+
+export function loadHistory(): DecisionRecord[] {
+  if (typeof window === "undefined") return [];
+  return (read(OWNER_REVIEW_KEY) ?? read(OWNER_REVIEW_BACKUP_KEY))?.history ?? [];
+}
+
+function write(history: DecisionRecord[]) {
+  const data: Stored = { version: 1, history };
+  const json = JSON.stringify(data);
+  // Keep the previous good copy as a backup before replacing it.
+  const previous = localStorage.getItem(OWNER_REVIEW_KEY);
+  if (previous) localStorage.setItem(OWNER_REVIEW_BACKUP_KEY, previous);
+  localStorage.setItem(OWNER_REVIEW_KEY, json);
+}
+
+export function recordDecision(record: Omit<DecisionRecord, "at">): DecisionRecord[] {
+  const next = [...loadHistory(), { ...record, at: new Date().toISOString() }];
+  write(next);
+  return next;
+}
+
+/** Latest decision per item; earlier ones stay in the history. */
+export function latestDecisions(history: DecisionRecord[]): Map<string, DecisionRecord> {
+  const map = new Map<string, DecisionRecord>();
+  for (const r of history) map.set(r.itemId, r);
+  return map;
+}
+
+const csvCell = (v: string) => `"${v.replace(/"/g, '""')}"`;
+export function historyToCsv(history: DecisionRecord[]): string {
+  const head = ["itemId", "decision", "comment", "at", "revision"];
+  return [head.join(","), ...history.map((r) => head.map((k) => csvCell(String(r[k as keyof DecisionRecord] ?? ""))).join(","))].join("\n");
+}
+export function historyToJson(history: DecisionRecord[]): string {
+  return JSON.stringify({ version: 1, history }, null, 2);
+}
+
+/** Restore from an exported JSON file. Returns the merged history (deduplicated). */
+export function importHistory(json: string): DecisionRecord[] {
+  const parsed = JSON.parse(json) as Partial<Stored>;
+  if (!parsed || !Array.isArray(parsed.history)) throw new Error("Not a review export");
+  const valid = parsed.history.filter(
+    (r): r is DecisionRecord => !!r && typeof r.itemId === "string" && typeof r.decision === "string" && typeof r.at === "string",
+  );
+  const seen = new Set<string>();
+  const merged = [...loadHistory(), ...valid]
+    .filter((r) => {
+      const k = `${r.itemId}|${r.decision}|${r.at}`;
+      if (seen.has(k)) return false;
+      seen.add(k);
+      return true;
+    })
+    .sort((a, b) => a.at.localeCompare(b.at));
+  write(merged);
+  return merged;
+}
+
+export function download(name: string, text: string, type: string) {
+  const url = URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([text], { type }));
+  const a = document.createElement("a");
+  a.href = url;
+  a.download = name;
+  a.click();
+  window.setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 1000);
+}

new file mode 100644

+/**
+ * November 1 pencil and mark contract (STUDENT_INTERACTION_STANDARD.md,
+ * "Latest pencil and mark contract — 2026-11-01"). Review candidate B only:
+ * enabled with `?cursor=contract`; the default Workbook keeps current main behavior.
+ *
+ * One shared timing/colour source for the Workbook mark kernel and the single
+ * pencil-tip cursor, so activity families never re-implement their own timers.
+ */
+export const PENCIL_CONTRACT = {
+  /** Tip travels from the pointer to the first point of the mark. */
+  approachMs: 300,
+  /** One child's loop around a picture — about two seconds. */
+  circleMs: 2000,
+  /** Each X stroke; the pencil hops between the two strokes. */
+  crossStrokeMs: 850,
+  hopMs: 260,
+  /** Completed charcoal mark is held before the outcome colour appears. */
+  holdMs: 400,
+  /** Wrong mark shows red before the eraser touches down. */
+  redShowMs: 600,
+  /** Physical eraser sequence: touch down, rub, lift. */
+  eraseMs: 3500,
+  eraseEnterMs: 450,
+  eraseLiftMs: 450,
+  crumbCount: 12,
+  charcoal: "#3a3a38",
+  correct: "#258351",
+  wrong: "#c4320a",
+  minWidthPx: 3.3,
+  maxWidthPx: 6.4,
+} as const;
+
+export type ContractPt = [number, number];
+
+/** Smooth ease-in-out progress used for both the stroke reveal and the tip position. */
+export function contractEase(t: number): number {
+  const x = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, t));
+  return x * x * (3 - 2 * x);
+}
+
+/** Inverse of contractEase (monotonic), by bisection. */
+export function contractEaseInverse(p: number): number {
+  const target = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, p));
+  let lo = 0;
+  let hi = 1;
+  for (let i = 0; i < 32; i++) {
+    const mid = (lo + hi) / 2;
+    if (contractEase(mid) < target) lo = mid;
+    else hi = mid;
+  }
+  return (lo + hi) / 2;
+}
+
+function lengths(points: ContractPt[]): { seg: number[]; total: number } {
+  const seg: number[] = [];
+  let total = 0;
+  for (let i = 1; i < points.length; i++) {
+    const l = Math.hypot(points[i][0] - points[i - 1][0], points[i][1] - points[i - 1][1]);
+    seg.push(l);
+    total += l;
+  }
+  return { seg, total };
+}
+
+/** Point at fraction `t` of a polyline's length. */
+export function contractPointAlong(points: ContractPt[], t: number): ContractPt {
+  if (points.length < 2) return points[0] ?? [0, 0];
+  const { seg, total } = lengths(points);
+  let target = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, t)) * total;
+  for (let i = 0; i < seg.length; i++) {
+    if (target <= seg[i] || i === seg.length - 1) {
+      const k = seg[i] ? Math.min(1, target / seg[i]) : 0;
+      return [
+        points[i][0] + (points[i + 1][0] - points[i][0]) * k,
+        points[i][1] + (points[i + 1][1] - points[i][1]) * k,
+      ];
+    }
+    target -= seg[i];
+  }
+  return points[points.length - 1];
+}
+
+export interface ContractStroke {
+  points: ContractPt[];
+  /** ms after the mark starts */
+  begin: number;
+  dur: number;
+}
+
+export interface VelocitySegment {
+  points: ContractPt[];
+  begin: number;
+  dur: number;
+  /** CSS px; slow pencil = darker/wider, fast pencil = thinner. */
+  width: number;
+}
+
+/**
+ * Split a stroke into short pieces revealed in sequence. Each piece's width
+ * follows the pencil speed at that moment (3.3–6.4 px).
+ */
+export function velocitySegments(stroke: ContractStroke, pieces = 14): VelocitySegment[] {
+  const dense: ContractPt[] = [];
+  const per = 4;
+  for (let i = 0; i <= pieces * per; i++) dense.push(contractPointAlong(stroke.points, i / (pieces * per)));
+  const times: number[] = [];
+  for (let k = 0; k <= pieces; k++) times.push(contractEaseInverse(k / pieces) * stroke.dur);
+  const durs = times.slice(1).map((t, k) => Math.max(1, t - times[k]));
+  const fastest = Math.min(...durs);
+  const slowest = Math.max(...durs);
+  const { minWidthPx, maxWidthPx } = PENCIL_CONTRACT;
+  return durs.map((dur, k) => {
+    const slow = slowest === fastest ? 0.5 : (dur - fastest) / (slowest - fastest);
+    return {
+      points: dense.slice(k * per, k * per + per + 1),
+      begin: stroke.begin + times[k],
+      dur,
+      width: Math.round((minWidthPx + (maxWidthPx - minWidthPx) * slow) * 100) / 100,
+    };
+  });
+}
+
+/** Tip position (mark coordinates) at `ms` after the mark started, or null when off the mark. */
+export function contractTipAt(strokes: ContractStroke[], ms: number): ContractPt | null {
+  for (let i = 0; i < strokes.length; i++) {
+    const s = strokes[i];
+    if (ms < s.begin) {
+      if (i === 0) return null;
+      const prev = strokes[i - 1];
+      const from = prev.points[prev.points.length - 1];
+      const to = s.points[0];
+      const k = contractEase((ms - (prev.begin + prev.dur)) / Math.max(1, s.begin - prev.begin - prev.dur));
+      return [from[0] + (to[0] - from[0]) * k, from[1] + (to[1] - from[1]) * k];
+    }
+    if (ms <= s.begin + s.dur) return contractPointAlong(s.points, contractEase((ms - s.begin) / s.dur));
+  }
+  return null;
+}
+
+/* ── Single pencil-tip cursor choreography store ───────────────────────────── */
+
+export interface TipFlight {
+  id: symbol;
+  el: HTMLElement;
+  /** viewBox height of the mark (width is always 100). */
+  height: number;
+  strokes: ContractStroke[];
+  start: number;
+}
+
+let flight: TipFlight | null = null;
+let erasing = 0;
+const listeners = new Set<() => void>();
+const notify = () => listeners.forEach((l) => l());
+
+export function startTipFlight(f: Omit<TipFlight, "id" | "start">): symbol {
+  const id = Symbol("tip-flight");
+  flight = { ...f, id, start: performance.now() };
+  notify();
+  return id;
+}
+export function endTipFlight(id: symbol) {
+  if (flight?.id === id) {
+    flight = null;
+    notify();
+  }
+}
+/** While any eraser is active the pencil tip is hidden. Returns a release function. */
+export function holdEraser(): () => void {
+  erasing += 1;
+  notify();
+  let released = false;
+  return () => {
+    if (released) return;
+    released = true;
+    erasing = Math.max(0, erasing - 1);
+    notify();
+  };
+}
+export function getTipFlight() {
+  return flight;
+}
+export function isEraserActive() {
+  return erasing > 0;
+}
+export function subscribeTip(listener: () => void) {
+  listeners.add(listener);
+  return () => listeners.delete(listener);
+}
+/** Map mark coordinates to viewport px for a mark box (viewBox 0 0 100 height, meet). */
+export function markToScreen(el: Element, height: number, pt: ContractPt): ContractPt {
+  const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
+  const unit = Math.min(r.width / 100, r.height / height);
+  const ox = r.left + (r.width - 100 * unit) / 2;
+  const oy = r.top + (r.height - height * unit) / 2;
+  return [ox + pt[0] * unit, oy + pt[1] * unit];
+}
